Let's formulate correct LSE in p-space.

Correct L-BFGS objective details:
```
def _lse_output_from_y(y, beta):
    # y shape n
    z = y - np.max(y)
    ez = np.exp(z) # if range <= 700 due bounds; exp safe
    S = ez.sum()
    p = ez/S
    c = conv_self(p)
    dmax = c.max()
    zc = beta * (c - dmax)
    zc = np.clip(zc, -100, 100)
    e = np.exp(zc)
    sw = e.sum()
    w = e/sw
    L = dmax + np.log(sw)/beta
    val = 2*n*L
    # R via FFT
    N = next_pow2(3*n)
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    pr = np.fft.rfft(p_rev, N)
    wr = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(pr*wr, N)[:3*n-2]
    R = conv[n-1:2*n-1]
    T = float(w.dot(c))
    grad_y = 4.0*n * (p*R - T*p*p)
    # remove mean to reduce drift
    grad_y -= grad_y.mean()
    # nan to num
    return val, grad_y, p, R, T, c
```

```
def _run_lbfgs_y(y, beta, maxfun):
    y0=np.asarray(y,float)
    bnds = _y_bounds(y0) # list
    opts={'maxiter': max(50, maxfun//2), 'maxfun': min(maxfun, 2000?), 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':30}
    res = minimize(_softmax_lse_objective, y0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(beta,), bounds=bnds, options=opts)
    return res.x
```

    p=normalize
    best=p, best_score
    eta = initial based on variance
    t_iter=0
    for it:
       c=conv_self(p); cmax=c.max(); score=2n cmax
       active = indices where c >= cmax - tol*cmax, top k
       w uniform
       R = conv_r_w(p,w)

Minimize c_k(p+αd) subject sum d=0. c_k = t + 2α r·d + α^2 d*d? The quadratic term d*d_k. If d small, choose d=-r+mean to decrease linear. Maybe line search. Could be cheap.

However for one peak, reducing it may increase others. LSE handles multiple.

Maybe use pattern: Previous best's sequence may have active peaks spread over many lags. LSE high beta is apt.

Let's examine mathematical objective one more time for gradient in softmax. Use f(p)=2n M(p) where M=LSE/weighted. But f depends only p sum1. In y gradient: g_i = p_i(∂M/∂p_i) - p_i∑p_j∂M/∂p_j, times 2n. ∂M/∂p_i = 2∑w p_{k-i}=2R_i. ∑p_j∂M/∂p_j =2∑p_j R_j. What is ∑p_j R_j = ∑_k w_k ∑_j p_j p_{k-j} = ∑w c_k = T. So g=2n*p_i*2(R_i - T)=4n p_i(R_i-T). Yes.

For weighted objective with w fixed but target? Same formula with T_w. Good.

Exact max subgradient: If w over active with T_w = max (if all active max), same. Use LSE high beta where T≈max.

We should ensure gradient not too large. p~1/n, R~1/n, diff maybe a few 1/n, p*diff ~1e-9, 4n ~ 4e4 for n=1e4 -> 4e-5. Fine. L-BFGS steps.

Algorithm:
```
def _active_weighted_refine(p, deadline, max_active=32, max_phases=8):
    p=normalize(p); n=p.size
    best_p=p; best_sc=_exact_score(p)
    phase_time = max(5, (deadline-time)/max_phases)
    calib_t = _estimate_call_time(n)
    for phase in range(max_phases):
       if time > deadline - phase_time - 5: break
       c=conv_self(p); cmax=c.max(); sc=2n*cmax
       if sc < best_sc: update
       active = _active_indices(c, cmax, max_active=max_active, tol=1e-6)
       w = _solve_active_weights(p,c,cmax,active)
       # quick check stationarity: R,T; err
       # run weighted L-BFGS with maxfun based on phase_time and maybe multiplier [0.5,1,1.5]
       y=to_y(p)
       for maxfun_factor in [0.25,0.6,1.0]:?
           maxfun = int(min(300, (phase_dead - now)*0.5 / tper * factor))
           y2=_run_lbfgs_weighted(y,w,maxfun)
           p2=p_from_y(y2)
           sc2=exact
           if sc2 < best_sc*(1-1e-10): accept p=p2; best
           else: p=p2? maybe if sc2 <= sc+1e-8? 
       # recompute active next
    return best
```

`_solve_active_weights(p,c,cmax,active)`:

     else:

        end=n
        seg_len=end-start
        # for i from start to n-1, j=k-i from n-1 down to k-n+1? length seg_len
        # j = k - i. i=start -> j=n-1; i=n-1 -> j=k-n+1.

        vals = p[n-1:k-n : -1]  # if k-n maybe? For k=n-1 (should go k<n branch), not.
        Q[start:end,col] = p[start:end] * vals
  - Check for k maybe < n covers all <=n-1; for k=n? else.
  - rhs = cmax * p
  - If n large and m maybe 32, lsq_linear could be memory; OK.

`_fast_self_conv(a)`:
```
n=a.size; if n==0 return empty; M=2*n-1; N=next_pow2(M); A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]; return np.maximum(out,0)
```
Need ensure if a length n, `irfft` returns length N; good.

`_r_convolve(p,w)`: If w length 2n-1.
```
n=p.size; N=next_pow2(3*n-1?) 3*n? use 3*n
pr=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
wr=np.fft.rfft(w, N)
conv=np.fft.irfft(pr*wr,N)
return conv[n-1:2*n-1]

def _active_weighted_refine(p0, deadline, max_phases=6, max_active=32):
    p=normalize(p0); n=p.size
    best_p=p.copy(); best_sc=_exact_score(p)
    if not finite: return best
    t_per=_estimate_call_time(n)
    phase_deadline_start=time.monotonic()
    total_rem=deadline-phase_deadline_start
    if total_rem<20: return best
    phase_basetime=total_rem/max(max_phases,1)
    for phase in range(max_phases):
       phase_dead=phase_deadline_start + (phase+1)*phase_basetime
       if time > phase_dead - 10: break
       c=_fast_self_conv(p); cmax=float(c.max()); sc=2n*cmax
       if finite and sc<best_sc: best_p=p.copy(); best_sc=sc
       active=_active_indices(c,cmax,max_active=max_active,rel_tol=1e-5)
       try:
          w=_solve_active_weights(p,c,cmax,active)
       except: w=ones/len active? 
       # Run weighted lbfgs for maybe 3 subruns
       subrem=phase_dead-time
       if subrem<10: continue
       y=_to_y(p)
       # factors [0.3,0.4,0.3]
       for fac in [0.3,0.4,0.3]:
          maxfun=int(max(20, min(300, subrem*fac/t_per)))
          if time > phase_dead-5: break
          y2=_run_lbfgs_weighted(y,w, maxfun)
          p2=_p_from_y(y2)
          sc2=_exact_score(p2)
          if finite:
             if sc2 < best_sc: best_sc=sc2; best_p=p2
             # accept p2 if sc2 <= best_sc*(1+1e-7) else keep best? For active, set p=best_p? 
             if sc2 <= best_sc*(1+1e-6): p=p2
             else: p=best_p
          else: p=best_p
       # after subruns, if p not best maybe continue from best
       p=best_p # recompute weights from best? Actually if p accepted, equals maybe best. Use best for next to avoid worse.
       # early exit if stationarity error small? Could check.
    # one final exact LSE high? not here
    return best_p,best_sc
```

Now `_active_indices`:
```
def _active_indices(c,cmax,max_active=32,rel_tol=1e-5):
   if c.size==0: return np.array([],int)
   if cmax<=0 or not finite: return np.array([np.argmax(c)],int)
   threshold=cmax*(1.0-rel_tol)
   idx=np.where(c>=threshold)[0]
   if idx.size==0: idx=np.array([np.argmax(c)],int)
   if idx.size>max_active:
      # Use partial argpartition for speed
      if idx.size <= 3*max_active: idx=idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[-max_active:]]
      else:
         top=np.argpartition(c, -max_active)[-max_active:]
         idx=top
      idx=idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]]
   # ensure argmax included
   am=int(np.argmax(c))
   if am not in idx: if idx.size<max_active: idx=np.append(idx,am) else replace lowest: idx[np.argmin(c[idx])]=am
   return idx.astype(int)
```
`np.argpartition(c, -max_active)` if max_active=0 no. If max_active <1? max.
Need `if idx.size > max_active` and max_active maybe 32. `np.argpartition(c, kth)` kth must be int. For top m: `np.argpartition(c, -m)[-m:]` works if m>0. `c[idx]` okay.
For membership `am not in idx` works for numpy array? returns bool. OK.

   pos=-1

   if pos>=0 and weights[pos] < 0.05:

       weights[pos]+=inc
       # reduce from largest other

Let's write `_run_lse_schedule` more concretely:
```
def _run_lse_schedule(p0, sub_deadline, label=""):
    p = _normalize(p0)
    n = p.size
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = _exact_score(p)
    t_stage = time.monotonic()
    if time.monotonic() > sub_deadline - 5 or best_sc == inf: return best_p,best_sc
    betas = _select_betas(n, sub_deadline - t_stage)
    if not betas: return best_p,best_sc
    stage_start = time.monotonic()
    stage_total = max(1.0, sub_deadline - stage_start)
    weights = np.arange(1, len(betas)+1, dtype=np.float64)
    # give more to high beta? arange increasing does.
    total_w = float(weights.sum())
    cm = 0.0
    tp = _estimate_call_time(n)
    if n <= 1024: cap=3000
    elif n <= 2048: cap=1800
    elif n <= 4096: cap=1000
    elif n <= 8192: cap=600
    else: cap=350
    for idx,beta in enumerate(betas):
        cm += float(weights[idx])
        beta_dead = stage_start + (cm / total_w) * stage_total
        beta_dead = min(float(beta_dead), sub_deadline)
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= beta_dead - 3:
            continue
        rem_sub = beta_dead - now
        if rem_sub < 8:
            continue
        maxfun = int(max(25, min(cap, max(30, (rem_sub * 0.75) / tp))))
        # If maxfun too small, maybe skip unless last
        if maxfun < 30 and idx != len(betas)-1:
            continue
        p2 = _run_lbfgs_lse(p, float(beta), maxfun)
        sc2 = _exact_score(p2)
        if np.isfinite(sc2):
            if sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc2; best_p = p2.copy()
            allow = 3e-6 * best_sc if idx < len(betas)-1 else 0.0
            print? maybe only if improved.
            if sc2 <= best_sc + allow:
                p = p2.copy()
        else:
            p = best_p
        if time.monotonic() >= sub_deadline - 3:
            break
    # final if p not exact best, maybe one high beta? no
    return best_p, best_sc
```
But `cm +=` inside and `stage_start` should be fixed. `cm` starts 0. Good.

```
def _select_betas(n, rem):
    rem=float(max(0,rem))
    scale=max(1.0, n/4096.0)
    base=[10,40,160,640,2560,10240,40960,163840]
    betas=[]
    for b in base:
       b2=int(b*scale)
       if b2 not in betas: betas.append(b2)
    if rem > 120:
       for b in [500000, 2000000]:
          b2=int(b*scale)
          if b2 not in betas and b2<20000000: betas.append(b2)
    if rem > 300:
       b2=int(5000000*scale)
       if b2 not in betas and b2<20000000: betas.append(b2)
    betas=sorted(set(int(b) for b in betas if 1<=b<=20000000))
    if len(betas)>8:
       # keep smallest 3 and largest 5?
       if rem < 120: betas=betas[:4]
       elif rem < 300: betas=betas[:6]
       else: betas=betas[:-0?] # keep last 6? We can keep betas[-6:] plus one mid?
    # ensure at least 1
    return betas
```

Let's add helper `_binom_family(n, alpha)`:
```
def _binom_family(n, alpha):
   a=np.ones(n)
   for i in range(1,n): a[i]=a[i-1]*(alpha+i-1)/i
   a=np.clip(a,1e-300,None); return _normalize(a)
```

def _add_inits(inits, cap, n, arr):
    if len(inits) >= cap: return

    if arr.size != n: return
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    if arr.sum() <= 1e-300: return
    inits.append(_normalize(arr))

def _perturb_simple(p, rng, strength=0.1):
   p=_normalize(p); n=p.size
   noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
   q=p*np.exp(strength*0.5*noise)

This uses more time. For high n 600*0.04=24s. If sub_dead 100s, not all. Could set cap=1000 for high n to use 40s. Let's use:

```

```
def _active_weighted_refine(p0, deadline, max_phases=6, max_active=32):
    p = _normalize(p0)
    n = p.size
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = _exact_score(p)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc) or n < 64: return best_p,best_sc
    start=time.monotonic()
    rem=deadline-start
    if rem < 30: return best_p,best_sc
    phase_time=rem/max(float(max_phases),1.0)
    tp=_estimate_call_time(n)
    no_improve=0
    for phase in range(max_phases):
       phase_dead=start+(phase+1)*phase_time
       if time.monotonic() > phase_dead-5: break
       c=...; cmax...
       sc=2n*cmax
       if finite and sc<best_sc: best... no_improve=0; p=best_p
       else no_improve+=1? Need if p same.
       if no_improve>=3: break
       active=_active_indices(c,cmax,max_active=max_active,rel_tol=1e-4)
       w=_solve_active_weights(p,c,cmax,active)
       y=_to_y(p)
       sub_dead=phase_dead-5
       # 3 subruns fractions 0.3,0.4,0.3
       facs=(0.30,0.40,0.30)
       prev_sc=sc
       for fac in facs:
          sub_dead2=phase_dead-3
          if time.monotonic() > sub_dead2-3: break
          maxfun=int(max(20, min(250, (sub_dead2-time.monotonic())*fac/tp)))
          if maxfun<20: break
          y2=_run_lbfgs_weighted(y,w,maxfun)
          p2=_p_from_y(y2); sc2=_exact_score(p2)
          if finite:
             if sc2 < best_sc: best_sc=sc2; best_p=p2; no_improve=0; y=_to_y(p2); p=p2
             elif sc2 <= sc * (1+1e-7): p=p2; y=_to_y(p2)
       # if no improvement in this phase, no_improve++
       if sc >= best_sc*(1-1e-12) and prev_sc >= best_sc*(1-1e-12): no_improve+=1
       # after phase, set p=best_p to continue from best? If we did, maybe lose. Use if no improve: p=best_p else p remains accepted.
       if no_improve>0: p=best_p
    return best_p,best_sc
```
This is messy. Simpler: for each phase, use p=best_p at start. Run weighted L-BFGS; if exact improves best, update and set p=best_p. If not, keep best. So no drift.
```
for phase:
   p=best_p.copy()
   c=...
   active=...
   w=...
   y=_to_y(p)
   for ...:
     y2=...
     p2=...
     sc2=...
     if sc2<best_sc: best=p2; y=_to_y(p2); p=p2
```
This repeats from best each subrun; no drift. Good. It may not explore but safe. Active phases likely yield tiny improvement.

If `w` solve expensive, do each phase. max_phases 5.

Need track time. Good.

Detailed function implementations:

`_next_pow2`:
```
def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s
```

`_resample`:
```
def _resample(a,n):
   a=_normalize(a); n=int(n); if a.size==n return a.copy(); if a.size==0...
   xold=np.linspace(0,1,a.size); xnew=np.linspace(0,1,n); out=np.interp(xnew,xold,a); return _normalize(out)
```

`_fast_self_conv(a)`:
```
def _fast_self_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    M=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]
    return np.maximum(out,0.0)
```

`_exact_score(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray...; n=size; s=sum; if s<1e-300 return inf
p=a/s
c=_fast_self_conv(p)
cmax=float(c.max())
return float(2.0*n*cmax)
```
Since p sum1, no S^2. If we call exact on raw parent before normalize? It divides.

`_p_from_y` as above.

`_to_y`:
```
def _to_y(p, low=_Y_LOW, high=_Y_HIGH): # default constants? default args okay no closure.
   p=_normalize(p)
   p=np.clip(p, 1e-300, None)
   y=np.log(p)
   return np.clip(y, low, high)
```
Top-level constants exist before function? Yes define constants before functions or defaults evaluate at definition; define constants first.

`_r_convolve_p_w`:
```
def _r_convolve_p_w(p,w):
   p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel(); w=np.asarray(w,float).ravel()
   n=p.size
   if n==0: return np.empty(0)
   if w.size != 2*n-1:
      ww=np.zeros(2*n-1)
      m=min(w.size, ww.size)
      ww[:m]=w[:m]
      w=ww; s=w.sum(); if s>0: w/=s
   N=_next_pow2(3*n)
   pr=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
   wr=np.fft.rfft(w, N)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(pr*wr, N)
   R=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
   R=np.maximum(np.nan_to_num(R,nan=0.0,posinf=0.0,neginf=0.0),0.0)
   return R
```
For n=1, N=4, conv length? slice [0:1], ok.

`_lse_from_y`:
```
def _lse_from_y(y,beta):
   y=np.asarray(y,float)
   n=y.size
   p=_p_from_y(y)
   c=_fast_self_conv(p)
   cmax=float(c.max())
   if cmax <= 1e-300:
      val=2.0  # uniform? fallback
      grad=np.zeros(n)
      return val,grad,p
   z=beta*(c-cmax)
   z=np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
   ez=np.exp(z)
   se=float(ez.sum())
   if se <=0 or not np.isfinite(se):
       w=np.zeros(2*n-1); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
   else:
       w=ez/se
   T=float(np.dot(w,c))
   if not np.isfinite(T) or T < 0: T=cmax
   val=2.0*n*T
   R=_r_convolve_p_w(p,w)
   grad=4.0*float(n)*p*(R-T)
   grad=np.nan_to_num(grad,)
   grad-=float(np.mean(grad))
   return val,grad,p
```
`np.nan_to_num` parameters maybe. Use `np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)`.

`_lse_objective`:
```
def _lse_objective(x, beta):
   try:
      val, grad, _ = _lse_from_y(x, float(beta))
      if not np.isfinite(val): return 1e10, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=float)
      if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): grad=np.nan_to_num...
      return float(val), grad.astype(float)
   except Exception:
      return 1e10, np.zeros(np.asarray(x).size, dtype=float)
```
Need zeros_like in except if x array. Could do in try.

   ...

`_run_lbfgs_lse(p,beta,maxfun)`:

def _run_lbfgs_lse(p,beta,maxfun):
   p=_normalize(p); n=p.size
   y=_to_y(p)
   maxfun=int(max(10,min(maxfun,20000)))
   if maxfun<5: return p
   bounds=_make_bounds(n)
   opts={'maxiter': int(max(20, min(500, maxfun//2))), 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-9,'maxls':30,'disp':False}
   try:
      res=minimize(_lse_objective, y, args=(float(beta),), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options=opts)
      y2=res.x if getattr(res,'x',None) is not None else y
   except Exception:
      y2=y
   p2=_p_from_y(y2)
   return _normalize(p2)
```
Could use `maxiter` cap 300 to avoid too many. If maxfun=20000, maxiter=500. Good.

   y=_to_y(p)

`_select_betas` no lambda:
```
def _select_betas(n, rem):
   rem=float(max(0, rem))
   scale=max(1.0, float(n)/1024.0)
   base=(5,20,80,320,1280,5120,20480,81920)
   betas=[]
   for b in base: ...
   if rem>120:
      for b in (300000,1000000): ...
   if rem>300:
      b=int(3000000*scale); ...
   if not betas: betas=[int(max(1, 100000*scale))]
   # unique sorted
   seen=set(); out=[]
   for b in betas:
      bi=int(b)
      if bi<1: bi=1
      if bi> _MAX_BETA: bi=int(_MAX_BETA)
      if bi not in seen:
         seen.add(bi); out.append(bi)
   out.sort()
   if len(out)>7:
      # keep first 2 and last 5
      keep=[]
      for b in out[:2]: keep.append(b)
      for b in out[-5:]:
         if b not in keep: keep.append(b)
      keep.sort()
      out=keep
   return out
```
No lambdas.

Now, active functions.

`_active_indices`:
```
def _active_indices(c,cmax,max_active=32,rel_tol=1e-4):
   c=np.asarray(c,float).ravel()
   m=c.size
   if m==0: return np.empty(0,dtype=int)
   if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax<=0:
      return np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
   max_active=int(max(1,max_active))
   threshold=cmax - max(cmax*float(rel_tol), 1e-300)
   idx=np.where(c >= threshold)[0]
   if idx.size==0:
      idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
   if idx.size > max_active:
      if idx.size <= 3*max_active:
          vals=c[idx]
          order=np.argsort(vals)
          idx=idx[order[-max_active:]]
      else:
          idx=np.argpartition(c, -max_active)[-max_active:]
      # sort by score ascending? not necessary
   am=int(np.argmax(c))
   # ensure am included
   found=False
   for val in idx:
      if int(val)==am: found=True; break
   if not found:
      if idx.size < max_active:
         idx=np.append(idx, am)
      else:
         # replace index with smallest c among idx
         j=int(np.argmin(c[idx]))
         idx[j]=am
   idx=idx.astype(int)
   return idx
```
When `np.argpartition(c, -max_active)` with max_active=m maybe returns all; if max_active=0 no. Good. If max_active > len(c)? no.

`_solve_active_weights`:
```
def _solve_active_weights(p,c,cmax,active):
   p=_normalize(p); c=np.asarray(c,float).ravel()
   n=p.size; mfull=2*n-1
   w=np.zeros(mfull,float)
   am=int(np.argmax(c))
   active=np.asarray(active,dtype=int).ravel()
   if active.size==0:
      w[am]=1; return w
   if active.size==1:
      w[int(active[0])]=1; return w
   # cap active to 40 and unique
   max_active=40
   if active.size>max_active:
      order=np.argsort(c[active])
      active=active[order[-max_active:]]
   # ensure am in active
   found=False
   for val in active: if int(val)==am: found=True; break
   if not found:
      if active.size<max_active: active=np.append(active,am)
      else:
         j=int(np.argmin(c[active])); active[j]=am
   m=active.size
   if m==1: w[int(active[0])]=1; return w
   Q=np.zeros((n,m),float)
   for col in range(m):
      k=int(active[col])
      if k < n:
         seg_len=min(k+1,n)
         if seg_len>0:
             vals=p[k::-1]
             Q[0:seg_len,col] = p[0:seg_len]*vals
      else:
         start=k-n+1
         end=n
         seg_len=end-start
         if seg_len>0:
             stop=k-n
             vals=p[n-1:stop:-1]
             Q[start:end,col] = p[start:end]*vals
   rhs=cmax*p
   weights=None
   if lsq_linear is not None:
      try:
         Aeq=np.ones((1,m),float)
         beq=np.ones((1,),float)
         res=lsq_linear(Q, rhs, bounds=(0.0,1.0), A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=beq, ftol=1e-8, max_iter=200)
         if res is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
             weights=res.x
      except Exception:
         weights=None
   if weights is None:
       weights=np.ones(m,float)/float(m)
   weights=np.nan_to_num(weights,nan=0.0,posinf=0.0,neginf=0.0)
   ws=float(weights.sum())
   if ws <= 1e-300:
       weights=np.ones(m,float)/float(m); ws=1.0
   weights=weights/ws
   # enforce max
   pos=-1
   for i in range(m):
       if int(active[i])==am: pos=i; break
   if pos>=0 and weights[pos] < 0.05 and m>1:
       inc=0.05-weights[pos]
       weights[pos]+=inc
       others=np.arange(m)
       mask=[i for i in others if i!=pos] # list comp maybe okay
       if mask:
          # find largest among mask
          best_i=mask[0]; bestv=weights[mask[0]]
          for ii in mask[1:]:
             if weights[ii]>bestv: bestv=weights[ii]; best_i=ii
          weights[best_i]=max(0.0, weights[best_i]-inc)
       weights=np.nan_to_num(...)
       ws=float(weights.sum())
       if ws>0: weights/=ws
       else: weights[pos]=1.0
   w[active]=weights
   ws=float(w.sum())
   if ws<=1e-300: w[am]=1
   else: w=w/ws
   return w
```
Use list comp `mask=[...]` maybe okay. Could use numpy boolean but list comp fine.
Potential memory: Q full n x m. For active m up to40, n=16384 ->655k 5MB. OK.

`_active_weighted_refine` as safe loop.

Now, `_make_inits` and `_binom_family`.

`_binom_family`:
```
def _binom_family(n, alpha):
   a=np.ones(n,float)
   for i in range(1,n):
      a[i] = a[i-1] * ((alpha + i - 1.0) / float(i))
   a=np.nan_to_num(a,nan=0,posinf=1e100,neginf=0)
   a=np.clip(a,1e-300,None)
   return _normalize(a)
```
If alpha small 0.05, factor (i-0.95)/i ~1, a decays slowly. For α=0? factor (i-1)/i, a0=1,a1=0? For α>0, positive. Use α list >0.05.

`_make_inits`:
```
def _make_inits(n,parent,rng,cap=50):
   inits=[]
   if parent is not None:
      pp=_resample(parent,n)
      _add_inits(inits,cap,n,pp)
      _add_inits(inits,cap,n,pp[::-1])
      # slight noise
      noise=rng.standard_normal(n)*0.05
      _add_inits(inits,cap,n,pp*np.exp(noise))
   # binom family
   for alpha in (0.2,0.35,0.5,0.65,0.8,1.0,1.3,1.8):
      a=_binom_family(n,alpha)
      _add_inits(inits,cap,n,a)
      _add_inits(inits,cap,n,a[::-1])
      _add_inits(inits,cap,n,a + a[::-1])
      if len(inits)>=cap: break
   if len(inits)<cap:
      x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
      _add_inits(inits,cap,n,np.ones(n))
      for alpha in (0.3,1.0,2.0):
         _add_inits(inits,cap,n,1.0+alpha*np.abs(2.0*x-1.0))
         _add_inits(inits,cap,n,1.0-alpha*0.5*np.abs(2.0*x-1.0))
      for f in (1,2,3,5):
         for amp in (0.3,0.8,1.5):
            _add_inits(inits,cap,n,1.0+amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*f*x))
            _add_inits(inits,cap,n,1.0+amp*np.cos(np.pi*f*x))
      for rate in (1.0,3.0,10.0):
         _add_inits(inits,cap,n,0.2+np.exp(-rate*x)+np.exp(-rate*(1.0-x)))
         _add_inits(inits,cap,n,0.2+np.exp(-rate*x))
         _add_inits(inits,cap,n,0.2+np.exp(-rate*(1.0-x)))
      # random smooth
      Nf=_next_pow2(n)
      for _ in range(6):
         freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)
         powv=1.0/((np.abs(freqs)*n+1.0)**float(rng.uniform(1.0,3.0)))
         powv[0]=0.0
         phase=rng.uniform(-3.141592653589793,3.141592653589793,size=powv.size)
         spec=powv*np.exp(1j*phase)
         noise=np.fft.irfft(spec,Nf)[:n]
         noise=noise-float(noise.mean())
         _add_inits(inits,cap,n,1.0+float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.2))*noise)
      for _ in range(4):
         _add_inits(inits,cap,n,0.1+0.9*rng.random(n))
   return inits
```
Need ensure expressions positive; `_add` clips. Exp noise may produce large, okay. For cos amplitude maybe negative if amp>1 and cos -1 ->0 if base1; positive. Good.

`_perturb_simple` as above.

Now, one more thought: The previous program's output likely already length 10240 and best 1.5064. Our code using parent will improve. But our low multi-scale might start from binomial and not use parent enough. Parent direct path and low init include parent resampled; main path likely starts from low best, which could be parent resampled if good. Good.

Could there be a risk that L-BFGS with softmax from a parent resampled to low loses high-score details and main path can't recover to beat direct parent? We have parent direct path and finalbest can be parent. Also low best if parent resampled.

Maybe direct parent route should be main not secondary. If parent exists and score 1.5064, optimize parent at high length likely improves. Our low path might take time but okay. Maybe allocate direct parent before main? We can schedule direct parent early to ensure improved. But parent high optimization maybe time heavy. Low path from parent resampled might also improve. Let's order:
1. Parent raw best.
2. Low search (includes parent) 22%
3. Direct parent high 12% (parent to parent_len) 22-34%
4. Main multi-scale 34-62%
5. Secondary 62-80%
6. final 80-100%
This ensures parent direct not starved.

Implement stage fractions based on usable = max(60,total-30).
- low_end=0.22*usable
- parent_end=0.34*usable if parent else low_end (skip)
- main_end=0.65
- sec_end=0.82
- final to 1.0.

Let's define:
```
usable = max(60.0, total_budget - 30.0)
t_base=t_start
low_end = t_base + 0.22*usable
if parent is not None:
   par_end = t_base + 0.36*usable
   main_end = t_base + 0.68*usable
   sec_end = t_base + 0.86*usable
else:
   main_end = t_base + 0.62*usable
   sec_end = t_base + 0.82*usable
final_end = t_base + usable # deadline
```
Then phases respect current time. If a phase is late, skip.

Parent direct target length: choose one near parent. If parent length 10240, use 10240. Could also if parent length<8192, use 8192. Use:
```
parent_len=parent.size

```
Run `_run_lse_schedule(ppar, min(par_end,deadline-60),'parent')`.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2

Algorithm:
- Work on the simplex (sum=1) using a softmax parameterization.
- Use a correctly derived softmax gradient for a log-sum-exp (LSE)
  smoothing of the max-convolution objective.
- Optimize progressively with L-BFGS-B at increasing LSE sharpness.
- Use multi-scale refinement: low-resolution global starts, then upsample
  and polish at larger lengths.
- Add structured initializations, including fractional-binomial profiles
  that are known to give low one-sided convolution peaks.
- Add a final active-weighted polishing stage (optional, conservative)
  that solves a small least-squares problem for active-peak weights.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

from scipy.optimize import minimize
try:
    from scipy.optimize import lsq_linear
except Exception:
    lsq_linear = None

_Y_LOW = -25.0
_Y_HIGH = 10.0
_MAX_BETA = 5000000.0
_CALIB = {}


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _resample(a, n):
    n = int(n)
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return _normalize(out)


def _fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:m]
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def _exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    if n == 0 or s < 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    p = a / s
    c = _fast_self_conv(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax)


def _p_from_y(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = y.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    z = y - float(np.max(y))
    ez = np.exp(z)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    p = ez / s
    return np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def _to_y(p):
    p = _normalize(p)
    p = np.clip(p, 1e-300, None)
    y = np.log(p)
    return np.clip(y, _Y_LOW, _Y_HIGH)


def _r_convolve_p_w(p, w):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    want = 2 * n - 1
    if w.size != want:
        ww = np.zeros(want, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(w.size, want)
        if m > 0:
            ww[:m] = w[:m]
        w = ww
        sw = float(w.sum())
        if sw > 0:
            w = w / sw
    N = _next_pow2(3 * n)
    pr = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
    wr = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(pr * wr, N)
    R = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    R = np.nan_to_num(R, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.maximum(R, 0.0)


def _lse_from_y(y, beta):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = y.size
    p = _p_from_y(y)
    c = _fast_self_conv(p)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 1e-300:
        return 2.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64), p
    z = float(beta) * (c - cmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    se = float(ez.sum())
    if se <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(se):
        w = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = ez / se
    T = float(np.dot(w, c))
    if not np.isfinite(T) or T < 0.0:
        T = cmax
    val = float(2.0 * n * T)
    R = _r_convolve_p_w(p, w)
    grad = 4.0 * float(n) * p * (R - T)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    grad -= float(np.mean(grad))
    return val, grad, p


def _lse_objective(x, beta):
    try:
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
        val, grad, _ = _lse_from_y(x, float(beta))
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            return 1e10, np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)
        grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        return float(val), grad
    except Exception:
        try:
            size = int(np.asarray(x).size)
        except Exception:
            size = 0
        return 1e10, np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)


def _weighted_from_y(y, w):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = y.size
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = _p_from_y(y)
    c = _fast_self_conv(p)
    sw = float(w.sum())
    if sw <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sw):
        w = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        if c.size > 0:
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w[0] = 1.0
    else:
        w = w / sw
    T = float(np.dot(w, c))
    if not np.isfinite(T) or T < 0.0:
        T = float(c.max())
    val = float(2.0 * n * T)
    R = _r_convolve_p_w(p, w)
    grad = 4.0 * float(n) * p * (R - T)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    grad -= float(np.mean(grad))
    return val, grad, p


def _weighted_objective(x, w):
    try:
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
        val, grad, _ = _weighted_from_y(x, w)
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            return 1e10, np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)
        grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        return float(val), grad
    except Exception:
        try:
            size = int(np.asarray(x).size)
        except Exception:
            size = 0
        return 1e10, np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)


def _estimate_call_time(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.01
    if n in _CALIB:
        return _CALIB[n]
    y = -np.log(float(n)) * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        _lse_from_y(y, 1000.0)
    except Exception:
        pass
    dt = time.monotonic() - t0
    if dt < 0.002:
        dt = 0.002
    if dt > 10.0:
        dt = 10.0
    _CALIB[n] = float(dt)
    return _CALIB[n]


def _make_bounds(n):
    return [(_Y_LOW, _Y_HIGH)] * int(n)


def _run_lbfgs_lse(p, beta, maxfun):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = p.size
    maxfun = int(max(10, min(maxfun, 20000)))
    if maxfun < 8:
        return p
    y = _to_y(p)
    bounds = _make_bounds(n)
    nit = int(max(10, min(maxfun, 500)))
    opts = {
        "maxiter": nit,
        "maxfun": maxfun,
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": 1e-9,
        "maxls": 30,
        "disp": False,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _lse_objective,
            y,
            args=(float(beta),),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options=opts,
        )
        y2 = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if y2 is None:
            y2 = y
    except Exception:
        y2 = y
    p2 = _p_from_y(y2)
    return _normalize(p2)


def _run_lbfgs_weighted(p, w, maxfun):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = p.size
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    want = 2 * n - 1
    if w.size != want:
        ww = np.zeros(want, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(w.size, want)
        if m > 0:
            ww[:m] = w[:m]
        w = ww
    sw = float(w.sum())
    if sw <= 1e-300:
        c = _fast_self_conv(p)
        if c.size > 0:
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w[0] = 1.0
    else:
        w = w / sw
    maxfun = int(max(10, min(maxfun, 5000)))
    if maxfun < 8:
        return p
    y = _to_y(p)
    bounds = _make_bounds(n)
    nit = int(max(10, min(maxfun, 300)))
    opts = {
        "maxiter": nit,
        "maxfun": maxfun,
        "ftol": 1e-14,
        "gtol": 1e-8,
        "maxls": 30,
        "disp": False,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _weighted_objective,
            y,
            args=(w,),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options=opts,
        )
        y2 = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if y2 is None:
            y2 = y
    except Exception:
        y2 = y
    p2 = _p_from_y(y2)
    return _normalize(p2)


def _select_betas(n, rem):
    rem = float(max(0.0, rem))
    scale = max(1.0, float(n) / 1024.0)
    base = (5, 20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480, 81920)
    raw = []
    for b in base:
        raw.append(int(max(1, b * scale)))
    if rem > 120.0:
        for b in (300000, 1000000):
            raw.append(int(max(1, b * scale)))
    if rem > 300.0:
        raw.append(int(max(1, 3000000 * scale)))

    out = []
    seen = set()
    for b in raw:
        bi = int(b)
        if bi < 1:
            bi = 1
        if bi > _MAX_BETA:
            bi = int(_MAX_BETA)
        if bi not in seen:
            seen.add(bi)
            out.append(bi)
    if len(out) == 0:
        out.append(int(max(1, 100000 * scale)))

    out.sort()
    if len(out) > 7:
        keep = []
        for b in out[:2]:
            keep.append(b)
        for b in out[-5:]:
            already = False
            for x in keep:
                if x == b:
                    already = True
                    break
            if not already:
                keep.append(b)
        keep.sort()
        out = keep
    return out


def _run_lse_schedule(p0, sub_deadline, label=""):
    p = _normalize(p0)
    n = p.size
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = _exact_score(p)

    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = float(sub_deadline - now)
    if rem < 8.0 or not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return best_p, best_sc

    betas = _select_betas(n, rem)
    if len(betas) == 0:
        return best_p, best_sc

    tp = _estimate_call_time(n)
    if n <= 1024:
        max_cap = 5000
    elif n <= 2048:
        max_cap = 2500
    elif n <= 4096:
        max_cap = 1500
    elif n <= 8192:
        max_cap = 900
    else:
        max_cap = 600

    stage_start = time.monotonic()
    stage_total = max(1.0, float(sub_deadline - stage_start))
    weights = np.arange(1, len(betas) + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    total_w = float(weights.sum())
    cum = 0.0

    if label:
        print("[{}] start n={} score={:.10f} betas={}".format(label, n, best_sc, len(betas)))

    for i, beta in enumerate(betas):
        now = time.monotonic()
        cum += float(weights[i])
        beta_dead = stage_start + (cum / total_w) * stage_total
        beta_dead = min(float(beta_dead), float(sub_deadline))
        rem_sub = beta_dead - now
        if rem_sub < 8.0:
            continue
        maxfun = int(max(20, min(max_cap, max(1.0, (rem_sub * 0.75) / max(1e-9, tp)))))
        if maxfun < 25 and i != len(betas) - 1:
            continue
        p2 = _run_lbfgs_lse(p, float(beta), maxfun)
        sc2 = _exact_score(p2)
        if np.isfinite(sc2):
            if sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc2
                best_p = p2.copy()
                print("[{}] beta={}: {:.10f}".format(label, beta, best_sc))
            allow = 3e-6 * best_sc if i < len(betas) - 1 else 0.0
            if sc2 <= best_sc + allow:
                p = p2.copy()
        if time.monotonic() >= sub_deadline - 3.0:
            break

    return best_p, best_sc


def _sort_by_score(pairs):
    m = len(pairs)
    if m <= 1:
        return pairs
    scores = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(m):
        scores[i] = float(pairs[i][0])
    order = np.argsort(scores)
    out = []
    for i in order:
        out.append(pairs[int(i)])
    return out


def _add_inits(inits, cap, n, arr):
    if len(inits) >= cap:
        return
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size != n:
        return
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    if float(arr.sum()) <= 1e-300:
        return
    inits.append(_normalize(arr))


def _binom_family(n, alpha):
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    alpha = float(alpha)
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * ((alpha + i - 1.0) / float(i))
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e100, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-300, None)
    return _normalize(a)


def _make_inits(n, parent, rng, cap=60):
    inits = []
    n = int(n)
    if parent is not None:
        pp = _resample(parent, n)
        _add_inits(inits, cap, n, pp)
        _add_inits(inits, cap, n, pp[::-1])
        noise = rng.standard_normal(n) * 0.05
        _add_inits(inits, cap, n, pp * np.exp(noise))

    for alpha in (0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65, 0.8, 1.0, 1.3, 1.8):
        a = _binom_family(n, alpha)
        _add_inits(inits, cap, n, a)
        _add_inits(inits, cap, n, a[::-1])
        _add_inits(inits, cap, n, a + a[::-1])
        if len(inits) >= cap:
            break

    if len(inits) < cap:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        _add_inits(inits, cap, n, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        for alpha in (0.3, 1.0, 2.0):
            _add_inits(inits, cap, n, 1.0 + alpha * np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0))
            _add_inits(inits, cap, n, 1.0 - 0.5 * alpha * np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0))

        for f in (1, 2, 3, 5):
            for amp in (0.3, 0.8, 1.5):
                _add_inits(inits, cap, n, 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x))
                _add_inits(inits, cap, n, 1.0 + amp * np.cos(np.pi * f * x))

        for rate in (1.0, 3.0, 10.0):
            _add_inits(inits, cap, n, 0.2 + np.exp(-rate * x) + np.exp(-rate * (1.0 - x)))
            _add_inits(inits, cap, n, 0.2 + np.exp(-rate * x))
            _add_inits(inits, cap, n, 0.2 + np.exp(-rate * (1.0 - x)))

        Nf = _next_pow2(n)
        for _ in range(6):
            freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)
            power = 1.0 / ((np.abs(freqs) * n + 1.0) ** float(rng.uniform(1.0, 3.0)))
            power[0] = 0.0
            phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=power.size)
            spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
            noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, Nf)[:n]
            noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
            _add_inits(inits, cap, n, 1.0 + float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.2)) * noise)

        for _ in range(4):
            _add_inits(inits, cap, n, 0.1 + 0.9 * rng.random(n))

    if len(inits) == 0:
        inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
    return inits


def _perturb_simple(p, rng, strength=0.12):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = p.size
    noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
    q = p * np.exp(0.5 * float(strength) * noise)
    q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
    return _normalize(q)


def _active_indices(c, cmax, max_active=32, rel_tol=1e-4):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
    max_active = int(max(1, max_active))
    threshold = cmax - max(float(rel_tol) * cmax, 1e-300)
    idx = np.where(c >= threshold)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
    else:
        if idx.size > max_active:
            if idx.size <= 3 * max_active:
                vals = c[idx]
                order = np.argsort(vals)
                idx = idx[order[-max_active:]]
            else:
                idx = np.argpartition(c, -max_active)[-max_active:]
        am = int(np.argmax(c))
        found = False
        for v in idx:
            if int(v) == am:
                found = True
                break
        if not found:
            if idx.size < max_active:
                idx = np.append(idx, am)
            else:
                j = int(np.argmin(c[idx]))
                idx[j] = am
    return idx.astype(int)


def _solve_active_weights(p, c, cmax, active):
    p = _normalize(p)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    mfull = 2 * n - 1
    w = np.zeros(mfull, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        w[0] = 1.0
        return w
    am = int(np.argmax(c))
    active = np.asarray(active, dtype=int).ravel()

    uniq = []
    for v in active:
        iv = int(v)
        if 0 <= iv < mfull:
            found = False
            for u in uniq:
                if u == iv:
                    found = True
                    break
            if not found:
                uniq.append(iv)
    active = np.array(uniq, dtype=int)

    if active.size == 0:
        w[am] = 1.0
        return w
    if active.size == 1:
        w[int(active[0])] = 1.0
        return w

    max_active = 40
    if active.size > max_active:
        vals = c[active]
        order = np.argsort(vals)
        active = active[order[-max_active:]]

    found = False
    for v in active:
        if int(v) == am:
            found = True
            break
    if not found:
        if active.size < max_active:
            active = np.append(active, am)
        else:
            j = int(np.argmin(c[active]))
            active[j] = am
    active = np.asarray(active, dtype=int)

    m = int(active.size)
    if m == 1:
        w[int(active[0])] = 1.0
        return w

    Q = np.zeros((n, m), dtype=np.float64)
    for col in range(m):
        k = int(active[col])
        if k < n:
            seg_len = min(k + 1, n)
            if seg_len > 0:
                vals = p[k::-1]
                Q[0:seg_len, col] = p[0:seg_len] * vals
        else:
            start = k - n + 1
            end = n
            seg_len = end - start
            if seg_len > 0:
                stop = k - n
                vals = p[n - 1:stop:-1]
                Q[start:end, col] = p[start:end] * vals

    rhs = cmax * p
    weights = None
    if lsq_linear is not None:
        try:
            Aeq = np.ones((1, m), dtype=np.float64)
            beq = np.ones((1,), dtype=np.float64)
            res = lsq_linear(
                Q,
                rhs,
                bounds=(0.0, 1.0),
                A_eq=Aeq,
                b_eq=beq,
                ftol=1e-8,
                max_iter=200,
            )
            if res is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                weights = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            weights = None

    if weights is None:
        weights = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)

    weights = np.nan_to_num(weights, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    ws = float(weights.sum())
    if ws <= 1e-300:
        weights = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
        ws = 1.0
    weights = weights / ws

    pos = -1
    for i in range(m):
        if int(active[i]) == am:
            pos = i
            break
    if pos >= 0 and weights[pos] < 0.05 and m > 1:
        inc = 0.05 - weights[pos]
        weights[pos] += inc
        best_i = -1
        best_v = -1.0
        for i in range(m):
            if i != pos and weights[i] > best_v:
                best_v = weights[i]
                best_i = i
        if best_i >= 0:
            weights[best_i] = max(0.0, weights[best_i] - inc)
        weights = np.nan_to_num(weights, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        ws = float(weights.sum())
        if ws > 0.0:
            weights = weights / ws
        else:
            weights = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
            weights[pos] = 1.0

    w[active] = weights
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 1e-300:
        w[am] = 1.0
    else:
        w = w / ws
    return w


def _active_weighted_refine(p0, deadline, max_phases=5, max_active=32):
    p = _normalize(p0)
    n = p.size
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = _exact_score(p)
    if n < 64 or not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return best_p, best_sc
    start = time.monotonic()
    rem = deadline - start
    if rem < 30.0:
        return best_p, best_sc
    phase_time = rem / float(max(1, max_phases))
    tp = _estimate_call_time(n)
    no_improve = 0
    facs = (0.25, 0.50, 0.25)

    for phase in range(max_phases):
        phase_dead = start + (phase + 1) * phase_time
        if time.monotonic() > phase_dead - 8.0:
            break
        p_use = best_p.copy()
        c = _fast_self_conv(p_use)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        cmax = float(c.max())
        active = _active_indices(c, cmax, max_active=max_active, rel_tol=1e-4)
        w = _solve_active_weights(p_use, c, cmax, active)
        y = _to_y(p_use)
        start_sc = best_sc
        for fac in facs:
            rem_sub = phase_dead - 5.0 - time.monotonic()
            if rem_sub < 10.0:
                break
            maxfun = int(max(15, min(300, max(1.0, rem_sub * float(fac) / max(1e-9, tp)))))
            y2 = _run_lbfgs_weighted_p(y, w, maxfun)
            p2 = _p_from_y(y2)
            sc2 = _exact_score(p2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc2
                best_p = p2.copy()
                y = _to_y(p2)
        if best_sc < start_sc - 1e-12:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 3:
                break
    return best_p, best_sc


def _run_lbfgs_weighted_p(y, w, maxfun):
    # Internal convenience; y is already logits.  This avoids another top-level
    # helper taking y directly while keeping the main weighted runner p-based.
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = y.size
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    want = 2 * n - 1
    if w.size != want:
        ww = np.zeros(want, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(w.size, want)
        if m > 0:
            ww[:m] = w[:m]
        w = ww
    sw = float(w.sum())
    if sw <= 1e-300:
        ww = np.zeros(want, dtype=np.float64)
        if want > 0:
            ww[0] = 1.0
        w = ww
    else:
        w = w / sw
    maxfun = int(max(10, min(maxfun, 5000)))
    if maxfun < 8:
        return y
    bounds = _make_bounds(n)
    nit = int(max(10, min(maxfun, 300)))
    opts = {
        "maxiter": nit,
        "maxfun": maxfun,
        "ftol": 1e-14,
        "gtol": 1e-8,
        "maxls": 30,
        "disp": False,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _weighted_objective,
            y,
            args=(w,),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options=opts,
        )
        return np.asarray(getattr(res, "x", y), dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return y


def _load_parent():
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    except Exception:
        h = None
    if h is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    if float(arr.sum()) <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return _normalize(arr)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        raw = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        raw = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(raw) or raw < 60.0:
        raw = 2400.0
    reserve = 30.0 if raw > 120.0 else 5.0
    usable = max(20.0, raw - reserve)
    deadline = t_start + usable

    try:
        seed_int = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed_int = 42
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed_int % (2 ** 32))

    parent = _load_parent()

    best_p = None
    best_score = np.inf

    if parent is not None:
        if parent.size > 30000:
            ppar = _resample(parent, 16384)
        else:
            ppar = parent.copy()
        sc = _exact_score(ppar)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            best_score = sc
            best_p = ppar
            print("Parent score: {:.10f} len={}".format(best_score, parent.size))

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        best_score = _exact_score(best_p)

    # ---- Low-resolution global search -----------------------------------
    n_low = 1024
    inits = _make_inits(n_low, parent, rng, 60)
    scored = []
    for arr in inits:
        sc = _exact_score(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, arr))
    if len(scored) == 0:
        scored.append((2.0, np.ones(n_low, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_low)))
    scored = _sort_by_score(scored)
    print("Best low-res init: {:.10f}".format(scored[0][0]))

    low_end = t_start + 0.22 * usable
    low_start = time.monotonic()
    low_candidates = scored[0][1].copy()
    low_results = [low_candidates]
    n_low_starts = min(3, len(scored))
    for i in range(1, n_low_starts):
        sub_dead = low_start + float(i + 1) / float(n_low_starts) * max(1.0, low_end - low_start)
        sub_dead = min(sub_dead, deadline - 40.0)
        if time.monotonic() > sub_dead - 20.0:
            low_results.append(scored[i][1].copy())
            continue
        p2, sc2 = _run_lse_schedule(scored[i][1], sub_dead, "low%d" % (i + 1))
        sc2 = _exact_score(p2)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
            best_score = sc2
            best_p = p2
        low_results.append(p2)

    low_pairs = []
    for p in low_results:
        sc = _exact_score(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            low_pairs.append((sc, p))
    if len(low_pairs) == 0:
        low_pairs.append((best_score, best_p))
    low_pairs = _sort_by_score(low_pairs)
    main_low = low_pairs[0][1].copy()
    if len(low_pairs) > 1:
        second_low = low_pairs[1][1].copy()
    else:
        second_low = _perturb_simple(main_low, rng, 0.15)

    # ---- Direct parent high-resolution path -----------------------------
    if parent is not None:
        par_end = t_start + 0.36 * usable
        if time.monotonic() < par_end - 30.0:
            target = 10240
            best_diff = None
            for L in (4096, 6144, 8192, 10240, 12288, 16384):
                d = abs(int(L) - int(parent.size))
                if best_diff is None or d < best_diff:
                    best_diff = d
                    target = L
            if target < 4096:
                target = 4096
            if target > 30000:
                target = 16384
            ppar = _resample(parent, target)
            scp = _exact_score(ppar)
            if np.isfinite(scp) and scp < best_score:
                best_score = scp
                best_p = ppar
            ppar, scpar = _run_lse_schedule(ppar, min(par_end, deadline - 50.0), "parent")
            scpar = _exact_score(ppar)
            if np.isfinite(scpar) and scpar < best_score:
                best_score = scpar
                best_p = ppar
                print("Parent direct best: {:.10f}".format(best_score))

    # ---- Main multi-scale path ------------------------------------------
    main_end = t_start + 0.68 * usable
    main_start = time.monotonic()
    main_total = max(1.0, main_end - main_start)
    levels = (2048, 4096, 8192, 16384)
    fracs = (0.10, 0.18, 0.26, 0.46)
    pmain = _resample(main_low, 2048) if main_low.size != 2048 else main_low.copy()
    cum = 0.0
    for L, fr in zip(levels, fracs):
        cum += fr
        sub_dead = main_start + cum * main_total
        sub_dead = min(sub_dead, main_end, deadline - 40.0)
        if time.monotonic() > sub_dead - 20.0:
            continue
        if pmain.size != L:
            pmain = _resample(pmain, L)
        pmain, scm = _run_lse_schedule(pmain, sub_dead, "main_%d" % L)
        scm = _exact_score(pmain)
        if np.isfinite(scm) and scm < best_score:
            best_score = scm
            best_p = pmain
            print("Main %d best: {:.10f}".format(L, best_score))

    # ---- Secondary exploitation path ------------------------------------
    sec_end = t_start + 0.86 * usable
    sec_start = time.monotonic()
    sec_total = max(1.0, sec_end - sec_start)
    psec = best_p.copy() if best_p is not None else second_low.copy()
    if psec.size < 4096:
        psec = _resample(psec, 4096)
    else:
        psec = _resample(psec, min(psec.size, 8192))
    levels2 = (4096, 8192)
    fracs2 = (0.35, 0.65)
    cum = 0.0
    for L, fr in zip(levels2, fracs2):
        cum += fr
        sub_dead = sec_start + cum * sec_total
        sub_dead = min(sub_dead, sec_end, deadline - 40.0)
        if time.monotonic() > sub_dead - 20.0:
            continue
        if psec.size != L:
            psec = _resample(psec, L)
        psec, scs = _run_lse_schedule(psec, sub_dead, "sec_%d" % L)
        scs = _exact_score(psec)
        if np.isfinite(scs) and scs < best_score:
            best_score = scs
            best_p = psec
            print("Secondary %d best: {:.10f}".format(L, best_score))

    # ---- Final active-weighted polish (conservative) --------------------
    if best_p is not None and best_p.size <= 24000:
        active_dur = min(300.0, max(30.0, 0.12 * usable))
        active_dead = min(deadline - 30.0, time.monotonic() + active_dur)
        if active_dead > time.monotonic() + 20.0:
            bpa, sca = _active_weighted_refine(best_p, active_dead, max_phases=4, max_active=32)
            bpa, sca = _normalize(bpa), _exact_score(bpa)
            if np.isfinite(sca) and sca < best_score:
                best_score = sca
                best_p = bpa
                print("Active-weighted polish: {:.10f}".format(best_score))

    # ---- Final upsample to 16384 if it can only help --------------------
    if best_p is not None and best_p.size < 16384:
        up_dead = min(deadline - 40.0, time.monotonic() + min(300.0, 0.12 * usable))
        if up_dead > time.monotonic() + 30.0:
            pup = _resample(best_p, 16384)
            pup, scu = _run_lse_schedule(pup, up_dead, "upsample_16384")
            scu = _exact_score(pup)
            if np.isfinite(scu) and scu < best_score:
                best_score = scu
                best_p = pup
                print("Upsampled to 16384: {:.10f}".format(best_score))

    # ---- Final direct polish on current best ----------------------------
    if best_p is not None:
        final_dead = min(deadline - 10.0, time.monotonic() + max(30.0, 0.08 * usable))
        if final_dead > time.monotonic() + 15.0:
            bp, scf = _run_lse_schedule(best_p, final_dead, "final")
            scf = _exact_score(bp)
            if np.isfinite(scf) and scf < best_score:
                best_score = scf
                best_p = bp
                print("Final polish: {:.10f}".format(best_score))

    # ---- Build valid output ---------------------------------------------
    if best_p is None:
        best_p = np.full(1024, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)

    arr = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        arr = np.full(1024, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
        s = 0.0
    if s >= 0.01:
        arr = arr / s
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    out = []
    for v in arr:
        out.append(float(v))

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = float(best_score)

    print(
        "FINAL internal={:.10f} verified={:.10f} len={} time={:.1f}s".format(
            float(best_score), verified, len(out), time.monotonic() - t_start
        )
    )
    return out
```
